Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the latin object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 604. The moduli space has dimension at most 750 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_604(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 604. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^750(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on latin structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 791. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 354 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by latin generators.
